- **spielbuch-monitor**: Datengetriggerter Agent, der das Abweichungs-Log überwacht und Playbook- Aktualisierungen vorschlägt, sobald eine Klauselposition häufig genug überschritten wurde, um auf eine Diskrepanz zwischen Playbook und gelebter Praxis hinzudeuten. Standardschwelle: 5 Abweichungen zur selben Klausel innerhalb eines rollierenden 12-Monats-Fensters (konfigurierbar in `~/.claude/plugins/config/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht/vertragsrecht/CLAUDE.md`). Auslöser: "Playbook prüfen", "Playbook-Aktualisierungen", "Playbook-Monitor" oder automatisch nach jedem Deal-Debrief-Lauf. ([vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim](https://github.com/vincentagwu/deutsches-recht-slim/blob/HEAD/vertragsrecht/agents/spielbuch-monitor.md))
- **Spike**: Networking and routing — DNS configuration, load balancing, service mesh, traffic management ([tmcleod3/voidforge](https://github.com/tmcleod3/voidforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spike-routing.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **spike**: Bounded investigation that answers one specific technical unknown so a blocked task can resume. Returns a finding, never an implementation. Dispatch from aikit:handling-blockers when the failure routes downward. ([Athyrr/aikit](https://github.com/Athyrr/aikit/blob/HEAD/agents/spike.md))
- **spike**: Writes throwaway code to answer 1 question about a library, an API, or a performance limit. Use when a plan or a research option rests on an unverified assumption. ([liaden/dotfiles](https://github.com/liaden/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spike.md))
- **spike**: Measurement or research spike that must end with numbers and a recommendation, never with a PR. Foreground measurements with bounded timeouts only. ([MasonRhodesDev/pm-loop](https://github.com/MasonRhodesDev/pm-loop/blob/HEAD/agents/spike.md))
- **spike**: Technical investigation agent. Answers specific feasibility questions with evidence before committing to implementation. Use when there is genuine technical uncertainty: unknown API behavior, untested integration, library capability, performance characteristic, or architecture viability. Researches internally (codebase) then externally (web), experiments if needed in isolated worktree, returns structured findings report. ([rmolines/context-engineering](https://github.com/rmolines/context-engineering/blob/HEAD/agents/spike.md))
- **spike-adversarial-verifier**: Challenges the spike synthesis output adversarially. Spawned by @spike-orchestrator after synthesis completes. Finds what the writers assumed, what wasn't checked, and what would invalidate the recommendation. Works against the post-reflect output. Never writes the dossier or slice. ([tannerolsen7/agent-harness](https://github.com/tannerolsen7/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spike-adversarial-verifier.md) · 2 copies)
- **spike-agent**: Throwaway validation of a feature's open questions before spec work. Writes only under features/&lt;id&gt;/spike/. Distills results into findings.md plus a small reference implementation. Not for features with no open questions, and never for code intended to be merged. ([Davidcparrar/harness-sdd](https://github.com/Davidcparrar/harness-sdd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spike-agent.md))
- **spike-investigator**: Hands-on exploration agent that experiments freely to answer a specific technical hypothesis. Produces evidence, not production code. ([yuriyvsavchuk/devflow](https://github.com/yuriyvsavchuk/devflow/blob/HEAD/agents/spike-investigator.agent.md) · ★1)
- **spike-orchestrator**: Orchestrates the full spike pipeline for a research question. Spawned by /spike. Use when /spike is invoked. ([tannerolsen7/agent-harness](https://github.com/tannerolsen7/agent-harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/spike-orchestrator.md) · 2 copies)
- **spike-plan-review-agent**: Context-free PLAN.md sufficiency reviewer. - **spike-runner**: Autonomous owner of one feasibility spike inside `sandbox/`. Runs the entire loop — scaffold, build, deploy if module, test, measure, report — without user intervention. - **spike-slice**: Writes the single TDD test that confirms or kills the spike recommendation. Spawned by @spike-orchestrator after verifiers complete.
